# Task
You will be given a **Requirement** and a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML. Your task is to annotate every operation listed in **Operations** with a specification.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pseudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Take the principle of modularization into account.
2. For each operation, determine its functional boundary based on the given requirement. Ensure high cohesion and low coupling.
3. If there is any unspecified details, such as undefined constants, rates, and ratios, then you must infer them.
4. Make sure that all requirements can be covered.

# Requirement
```text
To help the program chair, we are planning to develop a computer-assisted review system. The system will be available to users who can be authors, reviewers, and co-chairs. Each user has a name. Each user role (author, reviewer, or co-chair) has an id to identify the role. Papers are known by the title and whether they are research papers or experience report papers. The paper is reviewed by at least three reviewers. On the other side, the authors can submit as many papers as they want. When a reviewer logs in, she can consult the list of papers that have been assigned to her for review, and submit reviews for each of those papers. Each review has feedback as well as grade. Before submission the grade is UNDECIDED, and after submission it becomes ACCEPT or REJECT. A final decision is made by the co-chair for each paper.
Functional Requirement:
1.Calculate the number of unsubmitted reviews for a given reviewer. A review is considered unsubmitted if its grade is UNDECIDED. Return 0 if all reviews are submitted by the reviewer or if the reviewer has no assignments.

2.Before the chair makes the final decision, check whether all reviews for a paper (from at least three reviewers) are either exclusively Accept or exclusively Reject. Return a boolean value. If a paper has no reviews, or if any review is UNDECIDED, the reviews are not considered consistent (return false). The CoChair's makeFinalDecision method should return 1 and set the paper's decision only when all three conditions are met: at least 3 reviews, no UNDECIDED reviews, and all reviews are consistent; otherwise return -1 and leave the paper's decision unchanged.

3.Count the total number of papers submitted by an author.

4.Calculate the acceptance rate of papers for an author. Example: If an author submitted 10 papers with 6 accepted, return 0.60. If the author has no papers, return 0.00.

5.Convert a reviewer's submitted review grades into a numerical average score between 0.0 and 1.0, considering only submitted reviews (excluding UNDECIDED) and treating ACCEPT as 1 and REJECT as 0. For example, for 3 ACCEPT and 1 REJECT, the average score is (3×1 + 1×0)/4 = 0.75. The result should be within an error margin of 0.001. If there are no submitted reviews (all UNDECIDED or no assignments), return 0.00.


```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title conference Model

class Author {
    papers : List<Paper>
    calculateAcceptanceRate() : double
    countSubmittedPapers() : int
    submitPaper(paper : Paper) : void
}
class CoChair {
    makeFinalDecision(paper : Paper, decision : Grade) : int
}
class Paper {
    decision : Grade
    title : String
    type : PaperType
    reviews : List<ReviewAssignment>
    isAllReviewsConsistent() : boolean
}
class ReviewAssignment {
    feedback : String
    grade : Grade
    paper : Paper
}
class Reviewer {
    assignments : List<ReviewAssignment>
    calculateSubmittedReviewAverageScore() : double
    calculateUnsubmittedReviews() : int
}
class User {
    name : String
    roles : List<UserRole>
}
abstract class UserRole {
    id : String
}
enum Grade {
    UNDECIDED
    ACCEPT
    REJECT
}
enum PaperType {
    RESEARCH
    EXPERIENCE
}

Author --|> UserRole
Author --> "*" Paper : papers
CoChair --|> UserRole
Paper --> "*" ReviewAssignment : reviews  'opposite: paper
ReviewAssignment --> Paper : paper  'opposite: reviews
Reviewer --|> UserRole
Reviewer --> "*" ReviewAssignment : assignments
User *-- "*" UserRole : roles
@enduml
```

{org.eclipse.emf.ecore.impl.EOperationImpl@3a1f2a1 (name: countSubmittedPapers) (ordered: true, unique: true, lowerBound: 0, upperBound: 1)=Summary:
  Counts the total number of papers submitted by this author.
Algorithm:
  1. If this.papers is null, return 0.
  2. Return this.papers.size().
Related Features:
  - papers : List<Paper>  (the structural feature whose size is being counted)
Related Classes:
  None
Input:
  None.
Output:
  An int >= 0 equal to the number of papers the author has submitted.
Pre-condition:
  - The author instance is properly initialized; papers may be empty but should be non-null.
Pre-condition (input):
  - None.
Post-condition:
  - The author's state is unchanged.
  - The returned integer equals the size of the papers list., org.eclipse.emf.ecore.impl.EOperationImpl@697d6ded (name: makeFinalDecision) (ordered: true, unique: true, lowerBound: 0, upperBound: 1)=Summary:
  Attempts to set the final decision of a paper after verifying that the
  review process has produced a consistent verdict. Returns 1 on success
  and -1 if any required condition is not met.
Algorithm:
  1. If paper is null, return -1.
  2. If decision is null or equal to Grade.UNDECIDED, return -1.
  3. If paper.reviews.size() < 3, return -1.
  4. For each ReviewAssignment ra in paper.reviews:
       if ra.grade == Grade.UNDECIDED, return -1.
  5. If !paper.isAllReviewsConsistent(), return -1.
  6. Set paper.decision = decision.
  7. Return 1.
Related Features:
  - (Uses externally) Paper.decision : Grade   (the field to be updated)
  - (Uses externally) Paper.reviews : List<ReviewAssignment>  (used to validate)
  - (Calls externally) Paper.isAllReviewsConsistent() : boolean
Related Classes:
  Paper, Grade, ReviewAssignment
Input:
  paper    : Paper  - the paper for which the final decision is being made; must be non-null.
  decision : Grade  - the proposed final decision; must be Grade.ACCEPT or Grade.REJECT
                       (Grade.UNDECIDED is not a valid input here).
Output:
  An int value:
    -  1  if the decision was successfully applied to the paper.
    - -1  if any of the following is true: paper is null, decision is invalid,
          fewer than 3 reviews exist, at least one review is UNDECIDED,
          or the reviews are not all-consistent.
Pre-condition:
  - The paper must already exist in the system with its reviews populated
    (i.e., the review stage is complete).

# Task
You will be given a **Requirement**, a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML, and a list of operation specifications. Your task is to correct the specifications that are inconsistent with the requirement.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pseudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Read the requirement and the class diagram.

3. If there is any error, correct the erroneous specification.
4. Follow the specification format. Output changed specifications only; OMIT unchanged specifications.

# Requirement
```text
To help the program chair, we are planning to develop a computer-assisted review system. The system will be available to users who can be authors, reviewers, and co-chairs. Each user has a name. Each user role (author, reviewer, or co-chair) has an id to identify the role. Papers are known by the title and whether they are research papers or experience report papers. The paper is reviewed by at least three reviewers. On the other side, the authors can submit as many papers as they want. When a reviewer logs in, she can consult the list of papers that have been assigned to her for review, and submit reviews for each of those papers. Each review has feedback as well as grade. Before submission the grade is UNDECIDED, and after submission it becomes ACCEPT or REJECT. A final decision is made by the co-chair for each paper.
Functional Requirement:
1.Calculate the number of unsubmitted reviews for a given reviewer. A review is considered unsubmitted if its grade is UNDECIDED. Return 0 if all reviews are submitted by the reviewer or if the reviewer has no assignments.

2.Before the chair makes the final decision, check whether all reviews for a paper (from at least three reviewers) are either exclusively Accept or exclusively Reject. Return a boolean value. If a paper has no reviews, or if any review is UNDECIDED, the reviews are not considered consistent (return false). The CoChair's makeFinalDecision method should return 1 and set the paper's decision only when all three conditions are met: at least 3 reviews, no UNDECIDED reviews, and all reviews are consistent; otherwise return -1 and leave the paper's decision unchanged.

3.Count the total number of papers submitted by an author.

4.Calculate the acceptance rate of papers for an author. Example: If an author submitted 10 papers with 6 accepted, return 0.60. If the author has no papers, return 0.00.

5.Convert a reviewer's submitted review grades into a numerical average score between 0.0 and 1.0, considering only submitted reviews (excluding UNDECIDED) and treating ACCEPT as 1 and REJECT as 0. For example, for 3 ACCEPT and 1 REJECT, the average score is (3×1 + 1×0)/4 = 0.75. The result should be within an error margin of 0.001. If there are no submitted reviews (all UNDECIDED or no assignments), return 0.00.


```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title conference Model

class Author {
    papers : List<Paper>
    calculateAcceptanceRate() : double
    countSubmittedPapers() : int
    submitPaper(paper : Paper) : void
}

    makeFinalDecision(paper : Paper, decision : Grade) : int
}

    decision : Grade
    title : String
    type : PaperType
    reviews : List<ReviewAssignment>
    isAllReviewsConsistent() : boolean
}

    feedback : String
    grade : Grade
    paper : Paper
}

    assignments : List<ReviewAssignment>
    calculateSubmittedReviewAverageScore() : double
    calculateUnsubmittedReviews() : int
}

    name : String
    roles : List<UserRole>
}
abstract class UserRole {
    id : String
}
enum Grade {
    UNDECIDED
    ACCEPT
    REJECT
}
enum PaperType {
    RESEARCH
    EXPERIENCE
}

Author --|> UserRole
Author --> "*" Paper : papers
CoChair --|> UserRole
Paper --> "*" ReviewAssignment : reviews  'opposite: paper
ReviewAssignment --> Paper : paper  'opposite: reviews
Reviewer --|> UserRole
Reviewer --> "*" ReviewAssignment : assignments
User *-- "*" UserRole : roles
@enduml
```

# Operation Specifications
CoChair::makeFinalDecision
Summary:
  Attempts to set the final decision of a paper after verifying that the
  review process has produced a consistent verdict. Returns 1 on success
  and -1 if any required condition is not met.
Algorithm:
  1. If paper is null, return -1.
  2. If decision is null or equal to Grade.UNDECIDED, return -1.
  3. If paper.reviews.size() < 3, return -1.
  4. For each ReviewAssignment ra in paper.reviews:
       if ra.grade == Grade.UNDECIDED, return -1.
  5. If !paper.isAllReviewsConsistent(), return -1.
  6. Set paper.decision = decision.
  7. Return 1.
Related Features:
  - (Uses externally) Paper.decision : Grade   (the field to be updated)
  - (Uses externally) Paper.reviews : List<ReviewAssignment>  (used to validate)
  - (Calls externally) Paper.isAllReviewsConsistent() : boolean
Related Classes:
  Paper, Grade, ReviewAssignment
Input:
  paper    : Paper  - the paper for which the final decision is being made; must be non-null.
  decision : Grade  - the proposed final decision; must be Grade.ACCEPT or Grade.REJECT
                       (Grade.UNDECIDED is not a valid input here).
Output:
  An int value:
    -  1  if the decision was successfully applied to the paper.
    - -1  if any of the following is true: paper is null, decision is invalid,
          fewer than 3 reviews exist, at least one review is UNDECIDED,
          or the reviews are not all-consistent.
Pre-condition:
  - The paper must already exist in the system with its reviews populated
    (i.e., the review stage is complete).
  - The supplied decision must be Grade.ACCEPT or Grade.REJECT.
Pre-condition (input):
  - paper != null
  - decision != null && decision != Grade.UNDECIDED
Post-condition:
  - On success (return value 1): paper.decision equals the supplied decision.
  - On failure (return value -1): paper.decision is unchanged.
  - The co-chair's own state is unchanged.

/*
 * revised description for this operation_2

# Operation Specifications
Reviewer::calculateUnsubmittedReviews
Summary:
  Counts the number of unsubmitted reviews currently assigned to this
  reviewer, where an unsubmitted review is one whose grade is still
  Grade.UNDECIDED.
Algorithm:
  1. If this.assignments is null or empty, return 0.
  2. Initialize count = 0.
  3. For each ReviewAssignment ra in this.assignments:
       - If ra.grade == Grade.UNDECIDED, increment count.
  4. Return count.
Related Features:
  - assignments : List<ReviewAssignment>  (the structural feature whose
                                            entries are scanned)
Related Classes:
  ReviewAssignment, Grade
Input:
  None.
Output:
  An int >= 0 equal to the number of assignments whose grade is Grade.UNDECIDED.
  Returns 0 when the reviewer has no assignments, or when all assignments
  have been submitted (grade is ACCEPT or REJECT).
Pre-condition:
  - The reviewer instance is properly initialized; assignments may be empty.
Pre-condition (input):
  - None.
Post-condition:
  - The reviewer's state is unchanged.
  - The returned integer reflects the current number of UNDECIDED reviews.

Reviewer::calculateSubmittedReviewAverageScore
Summary:
  Computes the numerical average score of a reviewer's submitted reviews,
  where ACCEPT maps to 1.0 and REJECT maps to 0.0; UNDECIDED reviews are
  excluded from the computation.
Algorithm:
  1. If this.assignments is null or empty, return 0.00.
  2. Initialize sum = 0.0 and submittedCount = 0.
  3. For each ReviewAssignment ra in this.assignments:
       - If ra.grade == Grade.ACCEPT,  sum += 1.0; submittedCount++.
       - Else if ra.grade == Grade.REJECT, sum += 0.0; submittedCount++.
       - Else (ra.grade == Grade.UNDECIDED), skip (do not count).
  4. If submittedCount == 0, return 0.00.
  5. Return sum / submittedCount.
Related Features:
  - assignments : List<ReviewAssignment>  (the structural feature containing
                                           this reviewer's review tasks)
Related Classes:
  ReviewAssignment, Grade
Input:
  None.
Output:
  A double in the range [0.00, 1.00] representing the average score of
  submitted reviews, with an accuracy of at least 0.001.
  Returns 0.00 when there are no submitted reviews (all UNDECIDED or no
  assignments).
Pre-condition:
  - The reviewer instance is properly initialized; assignments may be empty.
Pre-condition (input):
  - None.
Post-condition:
  - The reviewer's state is unchanged.
  - The return value is a ratio of accepted reviews to total submitted reviews.

# Requirement
```text
To help the program chair, we are planning to develop a computer-assisted review system. The system will be available to users who can be authors, reviewers, and co-chairs. Each user has a name. Each user role (author, reviewer, or co-chair) has an id to identify the role. Papers are known by the title and whether they are research papers or experience report papers. The paper is reviewed by at least three reviewers. On the other side, the authors can submit as many papers as they want. When a reviewer logs in, she can consult the list of papers that have been assigned to her for review, and submit reviews for each of those papers. Each review has feedback as well as grade. Before submission the grade is UNDECIDED, and after submission it becomes ACCEPT or REJECT. A final decision is made by the co-chair for each paper.
Functional Requirement:
1.Calculate the number of unsubmitted reviews for a given reviewer. A review is considered unsubmitted if its grade is UNDECIDED. Return 0 if all reviews are submitted by the reviewer or if the reviewer has no assignments.

2.Before the chair makes the final decision, check whether all reviews for a paper (from at least three reviewers) are either exclusively Accept or exclusively Reject. Return a boolean value. If a paper has no reviews, or if any review is UNDECIDED, the reviews are not considered consistent (return false). The CoChair's makeFinalDecision method should return 1 and set the paper's decision only when all three conditions are met: at least 3 reviews, no UNDECIDED reviews, and all reviews are consistent; otherwise return -1 and leave the paper's decision unchanged.

3.Count the total number of papers submitted by an author.

4.Calculate the acceptance rate of papers for an author. Example: If an author submitted 10 papers with 6 accepted, return 0.60. If the author has no papers, return 0.00.

5.Convert a reviewer's submitted review grades into a numerical average score between 0.0 and 1.0, considering only submitted reviews (excluding UNDECIDED) and treating ACCEPT as 1 and REJECT as 0. For example, for 3 ACCEPT and 1 REJECT, the average score is (3×1 + 1×0)/4 = 0.75. The result should be within an error margin of 0.001. If there are no submitted reviews (all UNDECIDED or no assignments), return 0.00.


```

# Operation Specifications
Reviewer::calculateUnsubmittedReviews
Summary:
  Counts the number of unsubmitted reviews currently assigned to this
  reviewer, where an unsubmitted review is one whose grade is still
  Grade.UNDECIDED.
Algorithm:
  1. If this.assignments is null or empty, return 0.
  2. Initialize count = 0.
  3. For each ReviewAssignment ra in this.assignments:
       - If ra.grade == Grade.UNDECIDED, increment count.
  4. Return count.
Related Features:
  - assignments : List<ReviewAssignment>  (the structural feature whose
                                            entries are scanned)
Related Classes:
  ReviewAssignment, Grade
Input:
  None.
Output:
  An int >= 0 equal to the number of assignments whose grade is Grade.UNDECIDED.
  Returns 0 when the reviewer has no assignments, or when all assignments
  have been submitted (grade is ACCEPT or REJECT).
Pre-condition:
  - The reviewer instance is properly initialized; assignments may be empty.
Pre-condition (input):
  - None.
Post-condition:
  - The reviewer's state is unchanged.
  - The returned integer reflects the current number of UNDECIDED reviews.

Reviewer::calculateSubmittedReviewAverageScore
Summary:
  Computes the numerical average score of a reviewer's submitted reviews,
  where ACCEPT maps to 1.0 and REJECT maps to 0.0; UNDECIDED reviews are
  excluded from the computation.
Algorithm:
  1. If this.assignments is null or empty, return 0.00.
  2. Initialize sum = 0.0 and submittedCount = 0.
  3. For each ReviewAssignment ra in this.assignments:
       - If ra.grade == Grade.ACCEPT,  sum += 1.0; submittedCount++.
       - Else if ra.grade == Grade.REJECT, sum += 0.0; submittedCount++.
       - Else (ra.grade == Grade.UNDECIDED), skip (do not count).
  4. If submittedCount == 0, return 0.00.
  5. Return sum / submittedCount.
Related Features:
  - assignments : List<ReviewAssignment>  (the structural feature containing
                                           this reviewer's review tasks)
Related Classes:
  ReviewAssignment, Grade
Input:
  None.
Output:
  A double in the range [0.00, 1.00] representing the average score of
  submitted reviews, with an accuracy of at least 0.001.
  Returns 0.00 when there are no submitted reviews (all UNDECIDED or no
  assignments).
Pre-condition:
  - The reviewer instance is properly initialized; assignments may be empty.
Pre-condition (input):
  - None.
Post-condition:
  - The reviewer's state is unchanged.
  - The return value is a ratio of accepted reviews to total submitted reviews.

# Operation Specifications
Paper::isAllReviewsConsistent
Summary:
  Determines whether all submitted reviews for this paper are consistent,
  i.e., they are exclusively all ACCEPT or exclusively all REJECT.
Algorithm:
  1. If this.reviews is null or its size is 0, return false.
  2. If this.reviews.size() < 3, return false.
  3. Determine the grade of the first review (firstGrade). If firstGrade is
     Grade.UNDECIDED, return false.
  4. For each ReviewAssignment ra in this.reviews:
       - If ra.grade == Grade.UNDECIDED, return false.
       - If ra.grade != firstGrade, return false.
  5. If all checks pass, return true.
Related Features:
  - reviews : List<ReviewAssignment>  (the structural feature holding this paper's reviews)
Related Classes:
  ReviewAssignment, Grade
Input:
  None.
Output:
  A boolean:
    - true  if there are at least 3 reviews, none of which is UNDECIDED,
            and all share the same grade (all ACCEPT or all REJECT).
    - false otherwise (including when there are no reviews, fewer than 3,
            or any review is UNDECIDED).
Pre-condition:
  - The paper instance is properly initialized; reviews may be empty.
Pre-condition (input):
  - None.
Post-condition:
  - The paper's state is unchanged.
  - The return value reflects the consistency of the current set of reviews.

/*
 * revised description for this operation_2
 */

...

The CoChair's makeFinalDecision method should return 1 and set the paper's decision only when all three conditions are met: at least 3 reviews, no UNDECIDED reviews, and all reviews are consistent; otherwise return -1 and leave the paper's decision unchanged.

3.Count the total number of papers submitted by an author.

4.Calculate the acceptance rate of papers for an author. Example: If an author submitted 10 papers with 6 accepted, return 0.60. If the author has no papers, return 0.00.

5.Convert a reviewer's submitted review grades into a numerical average score between 0.0 and 1.0, considering only submitted reviews (excluding UNDECIDED) and treating ACCEPT as 1 and REJECT as 0. For example, for 3 ACCEPT and 1 REJECT, the average score is (3×1 + 1×0)/4 = 0.75. The result should be within an error margin of 0.001. If there are no submitted reviews (all UNDECIDED or no assignments), return 0.00.


```

# Operation Specifications
Author::countSubmittedPapers
Summary:
  Counts the total number of papers submitted by this author.
Algorithm:
  1. If this.papers is null, return 0.
  2. Return this.papers.size().
Related Features:
  - papers : List<Paper>  (the structural feature whose size is being counted)
Related Classes:
  None
Input:
  None.
Output:
  An int >= 0 equal to the number of papers the author has submitted.
Pre-condition:
  - The author instance is properly initialized; papers may be empty but should be non-null.
Pre-condition (input):
  - None.
Post-condition:
  - The author's state is unchanged.
  - The returned integer equals the size of the papers list.

Author::calculateAcceptanceRate
Summary:
  Calculates the acceptance rate of papers submitted by this author,
  defined as the ratio of accepted papers to total submitted papers.
Algorithm:
  1. If this.papers is empty (or null), return 0.00.
  2. Initialize acceptedCount = 0.
  3. For each Paper p in this.papers:
       if p.decision == Grade.ACCEPT, increment acceptedCount.
  4. Compute rate = acceptedCount / papers.size().
  5. Return rate as a double.
Related Features:
  - papers : List<Paper>  (the structural feature containing this author's submissions)
  - Paper.decision : Grade  (used to determine whether each paper is accepted)
Related Classes:
  Paper, Grade
Input:
  None.
Output:
  A double in the range [0.00, 1.00] representing the acceptance rate,
  rounded to a precision of at least 0.001 (e.g., 0.60 for 6 accepted out of 10).
Pre-condition:
  - The author instance is properly initialized with a non-null papers collection.
Pre-condition (input):
  - None (no external input).
Post-condition:
  - The author's state is unchanged.
  - The method returns 0.00 when the author has no submitted papers.
  - The method returns a value in [0.00, 1.00] otherwise.

Author::submitPaper
Summary:
  Submits a given paper and adds it to this author's list of submitted papers.
Algorithm:
  1. If the input paper is null, do nothing (or throw an IllegalArgumentException).
  2. Otherwise, append paper to this.papers (e.g., this.papers.add(paper)).
Related Features:
  - papers : List<Paper>  (the structural feature to which the paper is added)
Related Classes:
  Paper
Input:
  paper : Paper  - the paper object to be submitted; must be non-null
                   and typically must have a non-empty title and a valid PaperType.
Output:
  void
Pre-condition:
  - The author instance is properly initialized with a non-null papers list.
  - The supplied paper is not null.
Post-condition:
  - The supplied paper is added to the author's papers list.
  - The size of the author's papers list increases by 1.
  - No other author state is modified.

# Requirement
```text
To help the program chair, we are planning to develop a computer-assisted review system. The system will be available to users who can be authors, reviewers, and co-chairs. Each user has a name. Each user role (author, reviewer, or co-chair) has an id to identify the role. Papers are known by the title and whether they are research papers or experience report papers. The paper is reviewed by at least three reviewers. On the other side, the authors can submit as many papers as they want. When a reviewer logs in, she can consult the list of papers that have been assigned to her for review, and submit reviews for each of those papers. Each review has feedback as well as grade. Before submission the grade is UNDECIDED, and after submission it becomes ACCEPT or REJECT. A final decision is made by the co-chair for each paper.
Functional Requirement:
1.Calculate the number of unsubmitted reviews for a given reviewer. A review is considered unsubmitted if its grade is UNDECIDED. Return 0 if all reviews are submitted by the reviewer or if the reviewer has no assignments.

2.Before the chair makes the final decision, check whether all reviews for a paper (from at least three reviewers) are either exclusively Accept or exclusively Reject. Return a boolean value. If a paper has no reviews, or if any review is UNDECIDED, the reviews are not considered consistent (return false). The CoChair's makeFinalDecision method should return 1 and set the paper's decision only when all three conditions are met: at least 3 reviews, no UNDECIDED reviews, and all reviews are consistent; otherwise return -1 and leave the paper's decision unchanged.

3.Count the total number of papers submitted by an author.

4.Calculate the acceptance rate of papers for an author. Example: If an author submitted 10 papers with 6 accepted, return 0.60. If the author has no papers, return 0.00.

5.Convert a reviewer's submitted review grades into a numerical average score between 0.0 and 1.0, considering only submitted reviews (excluding UNDECIDED) and treating ACCEPT as 1 and REJECT as 0. For example, for 3 ACCEPT and 1 REJECT, the average score is (3×1 + 1×0)/4 = 0.75. The result should be within an error margin of 0.001. If there are no submitted reviews (all UNDECIDED or no assignments), return 0.00.


```

   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary:
   *   Attempts to set the final decision of a paper after verifying that the
   *   review process has produced a consistent verdict. Returns 1 on success
   *   and -1 if any required condition is not met.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. If paper is null, return -1.
   *   2. If decision is null or equal to Grade.UNDECIDED, return -1.

   *   6. Set paper.decision = decision.
   *   7. Return 1.

   *   - (Uses externally) Paper.decision : Grade   (the field to be updated)
   *   - (Uses externally) Paper.reviews : List<ReviewAssignment>  (used to validate)

   *   Paper, Grade, ReviewAssignment
   * Input:

   *   decision : Grade  - the proposed final decision; must be Grade.ACCEPT or Grade.REJECT
   *                        (Grade.UNDECIDED is not a valid input here).
   * Output:
   *   An int value:
   *     -  1  if the decision was successfully applied to the paper.
   *     - -1  if any of the following is true: paper is null, decision is invalid,

   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary:
   *   Attempts to set the final decision of a paper after verifying that the
   *   review process has produced a consistent verdict. Returns 1 on success
   *   and -1 if any required condition is not met.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. If paper is null, return -1.
   *   2. If decision is null or equal to Grade.UNDECIDED, return -1.
   *   3. If paper.reviews.size() < 3, return -1.
   *   4. For each ReviewAssignment ra in paper.reviews:
   *        if ra.grade == Grade.UNDECIDED, return -1.
   *   5. If !paper.isAllReviewsConsistent(), return -1.
   *   6. Set paper.decision = decision.
   *   7. Return 1.
   * Related Features:
   *   - (Uses externally) Paper.decision : Grade   (the field to be updated)
   *   - (Uses externally) Paper.reviews : List<ReviewAssignment>  (used to validate)
   *   - (Calls externally) Paper.isAllReviewsConsistent() : boolean
   * Related Classes:
   *   Paper, Grade, ReviewAssignment
   * Input:
   *   paper    : Paper  - the paper for which the final decision is being made; must be non-null.
   *   decision : Grade  - the proposed final decision; must be Grade.ACCEPT or Grade.REJECT
   *                        (Grade.UNDECIDED is not a valid input here).
   * Output:
   *   An int value:
   *     -  1  if the decision was successfully applied to the paper.
   *     - -1  if any of the following is true: paper is null, decision is invalid,
   *           fewer than 3 reviews exist, at least one review is UNDECIDED,
   *           or the reviews are not all-consistent.
   * Pre-condition:
   *   - The paper must already exist in the system with its reviews populated
   *     (i.e., the review stage is complete).
   *   - The supplied decision must be Grade.ACCEPT or Grade.REJECT.
   * Pre-condition (input):
   *   - paper != null
   *   - decision != null && decision != Grade.UNDECIDED
   * Post-condition:
   *   - On success (return value 1): paper.decision equals the supplied decision.
   *   - On failure (return value -1): paper.decision is unchanged.
   *   - The co-chair's own state is unchanged.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated
   */

   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary:
   *   Attempts to set the final decision of a paper after verifying that the
   *   review process has produced a consistent verdict. Returns 1 on success
   *   and -1 if any required condition is not met.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. If paper is null, return -1.
   *   2. If decision is null or equal to Grade.UNDECIDED, return -1.
   *   3. If paper.reviews.size() < 3, return -1.
   *   4. For each ReviewAssignment ra in paper.reviews:
   *        if ra.grade == Grade.UNDECIDED, return -1.
   *   5. If !paper.isAllReviewsConsistent(), return -1.
   *   6. Set paper.decision = decision.
   *   7. Return 1.
   * Related Features:
   *   - (Uses externally) Paper.decision : Grade   (the field to be updated)
   *   - (Uses externally) Paper.reviews : List<ReviewAssignment>  (used to validate)
   *   - (Calls externally) Paper.isAllReviewsConsistent() : boolean
   * Related Classes:
   *   Paper, Grade, ReviewAssignment
   * Input:
   *   paper    : Paper  - the paper for which the final decision is being made; must be non-null.
   *   decision : Grade  - the proposed final decision; must be Grade.ACCEPT or Grade.REJECT
   *                        (Grade.UNDECIDED is not a valid input here).
   * Output:
   *   An int value:
   *     -  1  if the decision was successfully applied to the paper.
   *     - -1  if any of the following is true: paper is null, decision is invalid,
   *           fewer than 3 reviews exist, at least one review is UNDECIDED,
   *           or the reviews are not all-consistent.
   * Pre-condition:
   *   - The paper must already exist in the system with its reviews populated
   *     (i.e., the review stage is complete).
   *   - The supplied decision must be Grade.ACCEPT or Grade.REJECT.
   * Pre-condition (input):
   *   - paper != null
   *   - decision != null && decision != Grade.UNDECIDED
   * Post-condition:
   *   - On success (return value 1): paper.decision equals the supplied decision.
   *   - On failure (return value -1): paper.decision is unchanged.
   *   - The co-chair's own state is unchanged.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public int makeFinalDecision(final Paper paper, final Grade decision)
  {
    if (paper == null)
    {
      return -1;
    }
    if (decision == null || decision == Grade.UNDECIDED)
    {
      return -1;
    }
    if (paper.getReviews().size() < 3)
    {
      return -1;
    }
    for (ReviewAssignment ra : paper.getReviews())
    {
      if (ra.getGrade() == Grade.UNDECIDED)
      {
        return -1;
      }
    }
    if (!paper.isAllReviewsConsistent())
    {
      return -1;
    }
    paper.setDecision(decision);
    return 1;
  }

   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary:
   *   Determines whether all reviews for this paper are consistent,
   *   i.e., there are at least 3 reviews, none is UNDECIDED, and they are
   *   exclusively all ACCEPT or exclusively all REJECT.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. If this.reviews is null or its size is 0, return false.
   *   2. If this.reviews.size() < 3, return false.
   *   3. Determine the grade of the first review (firstGrade). If firstGrade is
   *      Grade.UNDECIDED, return false.
   *   4. For each ReviewAssignment ra in this.reviews:
   *        - If ra.grade == Grade.UNDECIDED, return false.
   *        - If ra.grade != firstGrade, return false.
   *   5. If all checks pass, return true.
   * Related Features:
   *   - reviews : List<ReviewAssignment>  (the structural feature holding this paper's reviews)
   * Related Classes:
   *   ReviewAssignment, Grade
   * Input:
   *   None.
   * Output:
   *   A boolean:
   *     - true  if there are at least 3 reviews, none of which is UNDECIDED,
   *             and all share the same grade (all ACCEPT or all REJECT).
   *     - false otherwise (including when there are no reviews, fewer than 3,
   *             or any review is UNDECIDED).
   * Pre-condition:
   *   - The paper instance is properly initialized; reviews may be empty.
   *   - The operation takes no input parameters.
   * Post-condition:
   *   - The paper's state is unchanged.
   *   - The return value reflects the consistency of the current set of reviews.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model kind="operation"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean isAllReviewsConsistent()
  {
    if (reviews == null || reviews.size() == 0)
    {
      return false;
    }
    if (reviews.size() < 3)
    {
      return false;
    }
    Grade firstGrade = reviews.get(0).getGrade();
    if (firstGrade == Grade.UNDECIDED)
    {
      return false;
    }
    for (ReviewAssignment ra : reviews)
    {
      if (ra.getGrade() == Grade.UNDECIDED)
      {
        return false;
      }
      if (ra.getGrade() != firstGrade)
      {
        return false;
      }
    }
    return true;
  }
